# Agents · R · part 24

1,050 entries, researcher to researcher. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **researcher**: Senior исследователь (технологии и рынок), ТОЛЬКО ЧТЕНИЕ. Используй для сравнения технологий, библиотек, сервисов и API с источниками, проверки актуальных версий, лимитов, цен и ограничений, анализа конкурентов и рынка, сбора фактов перед решением архитектора или аналитика, а также для сбора фактов о зависимостях, версиях и интеграциях существующего проекта под конкретный вопрос (полный аудит «как есть» делает software-architect). Файлы не меняет — результат возвращает в отчёте. ([BobJustFry/tg-channel-audit](https://github.com/BobJustFry/tg-channel-audit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Pathfinder research specialist. Use for codebase analysis, dependency audits, prior-art discovery, and knowledge synthesis before implementation. Traverses knowledge graphs and grounds findings in live source code. ([Bonasib/crown](https://github.com/Bonasib/crown/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Answers one external question that the repository cannot settle — an RFC, a library behaviour, a version's documented contract — and records the answer with its sources as a single entry in `proj-console`. Use this when a plan, an implementation or a review turns on a fact that lives outside the repository, and never for a fact `AGENTS.md`, the code or the schema already settles. It isolates the network tool from every role that writes code. ([bookenjoyer67/agentic-console](https://github.com/bookenjoyer67/agentic-console/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Answers one external question that the repository cannot settle — an RFC, a library behaviour, a version's documented contract — and records the answer with its sources as a single entry in `proj-komun`. Use this when a plan, an implementation or a review turns on a fact that lives outside the repository, and never for a fact `AGENTS.md`, the code or the schema already settles. It isolates the network tool from every role that writes code. ([bookenjoyer67/komun](https://github.com/bookenjoyer67/komun/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Investigates codebase, docs, and external sources to gather facts with citations. Read-only stance for source code — no opinions, no recommendations. May write a single research output file when the invoking skill or caller specifies a target path; never edits source code. ([boosterai-pl/BoosterSite](https://github.com/boosterai-pl/BoosterSite/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Pesquisador e fact-checker do comitê de decisão (Decision Council). Único agente com acesso à web. Produz um rascunho independente ancorado em fatos verificáveis com fonte — limites de serviços, versões, preços, benchmarks, limitações conhecidas — em JSON estruturado. Use SOMENTE quando invocado por /decide, /decide-deep ou por pedido explícito do usuário, especialmente quando a decisão depende de fatos externos. ([boot-albuquerque/epistemic-roles](https://github.com/boot-albuquerque/epistemic-roles/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: **Adopt when:** evaluating libraries/services, comparing approaches, investigating unfamiliar tech, or answering "what's the best way to do X?" before a decision. ([bora168-coder/ai-engineering-os](https://github.com/bora168-coder/ai-engineering-os/blob/HEAD/.ai/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: You are a research agent. You can explore the codebase and search the web, but you cannot modify any files. ([bottah/agent-bobs](https://github.com/bottah/agent-bobs/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: 데이터 소스·외부 API·라이브러리를 조사하고 실측 근거와 함께 보고하는 조사가(프로젝트 파일 수정 안 함 — Write는 scripts/_tmp/ 일회성 스크립트 전용). Phase 001류 작업(데이터 소스 확보, API 제약 확인, 라이선스 검토)에서 사용. 독립적인 조사 여러 건은 병렬로 여러 인스턴스를 띄운다. 메인 agent는 결론만 받고 조사 덤프를 컨텍스트에 담지 않는다. ([Brad0329/bid-collectors](https://github.com/Brad0329/bid-collectors/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Cheap web researcher — searches/scrapes current docs and returns sourced summaries ([Bram-cat/my-pi-setup](https://github.com/Bram-cat/my-pi-setup/blob/HEAD/extensions/subagents/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Use before a decision that depends on material outside this conversation - product documentation, API references, competitor pages, standards, long PDFs, release notes. Returns a short summary with sources. Do NOT use for material already in the conversation, and do NOT use it to make the decision itself. ([brandonhall/claude-model-routing](https://github.com/brandonhall/claude-model-routing/blob/HEAD/plugins/model-routing/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Use PROACTIVELY to research documentation, APIs, frameworks, and best practices. MUST BE USED when user mentions: "documentation for", "how does X work", API reference, research, look up, find examples, best practices. ([brewpirate/ai-barf-notes](https://github.com/brewpirate/ai-barf-notes/blob/HEAD/Claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Web researcher — searches the web and synthesizes findings into a focused, sourced brief. Dispatch it whenever you're about to teach something you're not fully certain of (a fact, name, date, formula, definition, claim), and to scope a new topic's core concepts before planning a lesson. ([briangasca/learn-claude-code](https://github.com/briangasca/learn-claude-code/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Researcher — 深度调研 sub-agent。多源搜索、对比分析、方案调研。报告 + sources + 搜索日志全部落盘到 ./research/ 可追溯。 ([BrikerMan/git-github.com-BrikerMan-opencrew](https://github.com/BrikerMan/git-github.com-BrikerMan-opencrew/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Read-only investigator: reads the repository and the web, and reports what it found with sources. It cannot write files, run a shell, call MCP servers, or start other agents. Use it to look something up or map code before changing anything. ([brockshanson/agent-core](https://github.com/brockshanson/agent-core/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Badacz tylko do odczytu. Używaj, gdy trzeba zebrać fakty z kodu, dokumentacji lub sieci, zanim zapadnie decyzja. Nie modyfikuje plików. Każde twierdzenie podaje ze źródłem. ([BrokerBusiness-ai/claude-agent-kit](https://github.com/BrokerBusiness-ai/claude-agent-kit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher** ([bruna-dossantos/Bruna](https://github.com/bruna-dossantos/Bruna/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: 조사 전담 에이전트. 코드베이스 탐색, 버그 근본 원인 분석, 기술 선택지 비교를 수행한다. 산출물 생성이 아닌 "이해"가 목적이다. oh-my-gx:gx-tdd 구현 단계의 정체 에스컬레이션뿐 아니라 독립적으로도 호출 가능하다. &lt;example&gt; User: 로그인 실패가 간헐적으로 발생하는 원인 조사 요청 Agent: 가설 3개 수립 → git blame/log로 최근 변경 추적 → 세션 타임아웃 설정 변경이 원인으로 확인 → 조사 보고서 출력 &lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt; User: 결제 모듈의 구조 파악 요청 Agent: 진입점에서 시작하여 호출 체인 추적 → 레이어 구조, 핵심 클래스, 외부 연동 지점 정리 → 탐색 보고서 출력 &lt;/example&gt; ([bs-koo/oh-my-gx](https://github.com/bs-koo/oh-my-gx/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Explores a codebase to answer questions. Use when the user asks how something works, where something is defined, what calls what, or needs to understand unfamiliar code. Also use for broad searches where the answer might be in multiple files. ([bswrundquist/devtools](https://github.com/bswrundquist/devtools/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Read-only pre-implementation researcher for dependency evaluation, codebase pattern analysis, prior-art review, and technical context gathering. ([buenhyden/video-analysis](https://github.com/buenhyden/video-analysis/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Use for bounded external research, official-documentation lookup, technical comparisons, current API/tool verification, and evidence gathering whose browsing output would otherwise pollute the main Opus context. Return a compact evidence-backed synthesis. ([bugrackm/My-Agent-System-and-Skills](https://github.com/bugrackm/My-Agent-System-and-Skills/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Research topics via web search, documentation, and codebase analysis ([buisihung11/maxai](https://github.com/buisihung11/maxai/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/researcher.agent.md) · 2 copies)
- **researcher**: Research and documentation lookup - library docs, API patterns, guidance ([c-daly/agent-swarm](https://github.com/c-daly/agent-swarm/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: &gt; Intelligence officer. Finds signal in the noise. Never reports facts without telling you what to do with them. ([c7lavinder/xhaka](https://github.com/c7lavinder/xhaka/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Drives /autoresearch (base skill) with the team_adversarial preset and translates findings into a trading-strategy brief. ([cajias/nautilus-competition-run](https://github.com/cajias/nautilus-competition-run/blob/HEAD/competition_3/teams/team_adversarial/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Drives /autoresearch (base skill) with the team_balanced preset and translates findings into a trading-strategy brief. ([cajias/nautilus-competition-run](https://github.com/cajias/nautilus-competition-run/blob/HEAD/competition_3/teams/team_balanced/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Drives /autoresearch (base skill) with the team_breadth_first preset and translates findings into a trading-strategy brief. ([cajias/nautilus-competition-run](https://github.com/cajias/nautilus-competition-run/blob/HEAD/competition_3/teams/team_breadth_first/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Drives /autoresearch (base skill) with the team_completeness preset and translates findings into a trading-strategy brief. ([cajias/nautilus-competition-run](https://github.com/cajias/nautilus-competition-run/blob/HEAD/competition_3/teams/team_completeness/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Drives /autoresearch (base skill) with the team_depth_first preset and translates findings into a trading-strategy brief. ([cajias/nautilus-competition-run](https://github.com/cajias/nautilus-competition-run/blob/HEAD/competition_3/teams/team_depth_first/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Drives /autoresearch (base skill) with the team_speed_run preset and translates findings into a trading-strategy brief. ([cajias/nautilus-competition-run](https://github.com/cajias/nautilus-competition-run/blob/HEAD/competition_3/teams/team_speed_run/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Aufklärer. Kartiert eine bestehende Codebase, findet relevante Dateien, Konventionen, Build-/Test-Kommandos und externe Doku. Read-only. Nutzen vor jeder Planungs- oder Implementierungsphase. ([CallMeTechie/async-agent-team](https://github.com/CallMeTechie/async-agent-team/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Gather primary evidence across papers, web sources, repos, docs, and local artifacts. Use as a delegated evidence gatherer for research workflows (deep research, literature review, audits, replication recipes). ([cameronbracken/waterology-research](https://github.com/cameronbracken/waterology-research/blob/HEAD/.agent/agents/researcher.md) · 3 copies)
- **researcher**: Read-only investigator. Use for questions of fact about the codebase, a dependency, a market, or a failure. Returns findings with sources and named gaps. Never edits anything. ([canderson-maker/teardown-kit](https://github.com/canderson-maker/teardown-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Web research specialist. Fans out searches, reads primary sources, cross-checks claims, and returns a tight cited summary. Delegate any "look this up / compare options / what changed in X" task so the main session keeps its context. Read-only. ([canotalois/claude-kit](https://github.com/canotalois/claude-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Dış araştırma ajanı. Kütüphane/araç karşılaştırması, best practice taraması, rakip inceleme gerektiğinde kullan; bulgular .context/intel/ altına yazılır. ([Captain-jack0/QuestLog](https://github.com/Captain-jack0/QuestLog/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Gathers evidence for the research plugin's skills: searches the web and academic sources, reads primary sources and writes numbered evidence files with a URL behind every claim, or maps paper claims to file:line evidence in a locally cloned repository. Spawned by research:deep-research, research:literature-review, research:source-comparison and research:paper-code-audit for their evidence step. Use it when a research task needs sources gathered into a file, not for a quick lookup answered in the conversation. ([carelvniekerk/agentic-skills](https://github.com/carelvniekerk/agentic-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/research/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Parallel research worker for the research skill. Researches one or two commanders, strategies or themes and returns findings in the research.json contract. Use only when the research skill splits independent subjects across agents. ([Carlomos7/edh-companion](https://github.com/Carlomos7/edh-companion/blob/HEAD/plugins/edh-companion/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Use PROACTIVELY when you need to understand how something works before implementing. Explores codebase without polluting main context. ([CarmiBanxe/banxe-emi-stack](https://github.com/CarmiBanxe/banxe-emi-stack/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Looks outward after a solution is applied. Connects to the internet to find lessons learned, relevant research, and new lenses on the work. Use at the end of a task, after documentation. ([cassiusp-beep/arcade-lite](https://github.com/cassiusp-beep/arcade-lite/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Research and sourcing agent. Produces comprehensive research briefs with primary sources, statistics, and clickable URLs. ([cclawton/bullpen](https://github.com/cclawton/bullpen/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **Researcher**: Find relevant code related to the user's topic and explain existing patterns used in the codebase ([cderivan-lab/memory-mcp](https://github.com/cderivan-lab/memory-mcp/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/Researcher.md))
- **Researcher**: Research codebase and external sources to define goals, requirements, and constraints ([cdion/nex](https://github.com/cdion/nex/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/researcher.agent.md))
- **researcher**: 외부 라이브러리·SDK·API 공식 문서를 조사해 결론과 근거만 압축해 돌려준다. Spring Boot·소셜 로그인 provider·AWS 등 우리 코드 밖 지식이 필요할 때, 특히 2개 이상 문서를 비교해야 할 때 사용. ([Central-MakeUs/TripFit-server](https://github.com/Central-MakeUs/TripFit-server/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: 웹 리서치 워커 서브에이전트. 할당된 소주제에 대해 WebSearch→WebFetch로 신뢰 소스를 모아 출처 추적 가능한 구조화 findings(소주제 정리 + 주장↔소스 매핑 + 게시일·조회일 소스 목록)를 **텍스트로 반환**한다(파일을 쓰지 않음 → 메인 컨텍스트 보호). 보통 `research` 오케스트레이터 스킬이 소주제별로 병렬 디스패치한다. &lt;example&gt; Context: research 오케스트레이터가 "GraphQL vs REST 성능" 주제를 분해해 소주제를 위임 user: "소주제 'GraphQL over-fetching 실측 데이터'를 한국어로 조사해 구조화 findings로 반환해줘" assistant: "researcher 에이전트로 해당 소주제를 수집하겠습니다." &lt;commentary&gt; 오케스트레이터가 격리된 워커에 수집을 위임하는 전형적 상황 — researcher를 사용한다. &lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt; Context: 좁은 단일 주제라 오케스트레이터가 워커 1개만 디스패치 user: "'OpenAI structured outputs 공식 스펙'을 1차 자료 중심으로 조사해줘" assist… ([centurio1987/my-claude-plugins](https://github.com/centurio1987/my-claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/web-research/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Read `_protocol.md` in this same directory for shared rules before proceeding. ([chanhyeok-made/ccx](https://github.com/chanhyeok-made/ccx/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Read-only recon and research. Delegate - broad codebase search across many files, unfamiliar library/API/docs lookups, web research, collecting facts for a decision. Don't - design (architect), reviews (reviewer), edits, a lookup one grep answers. ([ChanningHe/dotfiles](https://github.com/ChanningHe/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Discusses research direction with the user, designs batches of experiments with clear hypotheses and success criteria, and analyzes finished results into a committed batch report. Never submits jobs and never merges to main. Use as the Researcher role of experiment-conductor. ([chaord-ai/chaord-marketplace](https://github.com/chaord-ai/chaord-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/experiment-conductor/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Performs open-ended web research. Searches the web and returns structured findings on a topic. Use for discovery rather than fetching a known page. ([ChaosAssembler/desert-city-states](https://github.com/ChaosAssembler/desert-city-states/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Explores a codebase to answer specific questions or build context for a new feature. Returns a structured summary. Use this when context-building would otherwise pollute the main session. ([Chawengwit/PPY](https://github.com/Chawengwit/PPY/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Read-only external research agent for official docs, releases, standards, and cross-source fact gathering. ([chemitaro/pyclassuml](https://github.com/chemitaro/pyclassuml/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/researcher.agent.md))
- **researcher**: Invoke when company or JD research is needed. Gathers company information, JD requirements, tech stack, etc. via web search. ([chenjingdev/chenjing-plugins](https://github.com/chenjingdev/chenjing-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/resume/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Investigate a question about the codebase. Read-only, reports findings. ([chewcorp/minime](https://github.com/chewcorp/minime/blob/HEAD/plugins/coder/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Read-only research. Returns structured brief with evidence and recommendation. ([chewcorp/rgh-market](https://github.com/chewcorp/rgh-market/blob/HEAD/plugins/rgh-mms/agents/researcher.md) · 2 copies)
- **researcher**: 외부 라이브러리·SDK·API 공식 문서를 조사해 결론과 근거만 압축해 돌려준다. 프레임워크 주 버전 차이·외부 로그인 provider 규격·클라우드 서비스처럼 우리 코드 밖 지식이 필요할 때, 특히 2개 이상 문서를 비교하거나 선택지를 모을 때 사용. ([chhezue/Superchaeyeons](https://github.com/chhezue/Superchaeyeons/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: You are a general-purpose profile discovery specialist. ([chmoder/upcaret-openclaw](https://github.com/chmoder/upcaret-openclaw/blob/HEAD/plugins/profile-research/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: You are a technical researcher. Your job is to investigate APIs, libraries, and technical approaches before they are used in the project. ([chris-cgsit/cgsit-claude-scaffold](https://github.com/chris-cgsit/cgsit-claude-scaffold/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Independent research subagent for SPADES. - **researcher**: Researches topics by selecting the appropriate method. Routes Python package research to pkgq MCP tool and other research to c3:research skill. - **researcher**: 웹을 탐색해 출처를 모으고 검증을 통과한 행으로 근거 지도를 만드는 연구자다. 호출 절차가 이름으로 지정할 때만 실행한다. ([chshin84/disciplined-coder](https://github.com/chshin84/disciplined-coder/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Researches technologies, libraries, best practices and open-source projects across docs, web and videos, and produces a concise research report. Use for technology selection or when current external information is needed. ([Chubby-Chocobo/photovault](https://github.com/Chubby-Chocobo/photovault/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Technical research on APIs, libraries, patterns, best practices. Use in parallel for investigating different topics before planning. ([chubedan4605/SMART_RESTAURANT_CBD](https://github.com/chubedan4605/SMART_RESTAURANT_CBD/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Investigates technical unknowns and writes findings to docs/RESEARCH/. Use when: external API questions, unfamiliar library usage, architecture trade-off analysis. ([Chulf58/FORGE](https://github.com/Chulf58/FORGE/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Niche and market research specialist, isolated from build context. Analyses Etsy search results, competitor listings, pricing strategies, and keyword opportunities. - **researcher**: Use when the user asks a general knowledge or research question unrelated to the current project -- e.g. "give me an overview of X", "how does Y work", "explain Z". Answers from training knowledge and web sources. Invoke via /research or @researcher. ([circld/dotfiles](https://github.com/circld/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/external/opencode/agents/researcher.md))

- **researcher**: Use when a ck-code skill needs external facts before deciding — a library's current version, idiomatic API usage, a migration note, a known bug or an approach comparison — gathered from context7, package registries and the web on a cheap model and returned as sourced findings, so the main agent never researches itself. ([ckandrinirina/ck-code](https://github.com/ckandrinirina/ck-code/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Use when a ck-code-lite skill needs external facts before deciding — a library's current version, idiomatic API usage, a migration note, a known bug or an approach comparison — gathered from context7, package registries and the web on a cheap model and returned as sourced findings, so the main agent never researches itself. ([ckandrinirina/ck-code-lite](https://github.com/ckandrinirina/ck-code-lite/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Investigate existing solutions before building. Use proactively before any new feature or product to avoid reinventing the wheel. ([cla1redonald/shipit-v4](https://github.com/cla1redonald/shipit-v4/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Research agent for external APIs, libraries, and documentation. Invoke before implementing code that touches external services. ([ClaudeTools/marketplace](https://github.com/ClaudeTools/marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/claudetools/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: External API and library research agent — checks the local codebase first, then fetches and verifies documentation before implementation begins. ([ClaudeTools/marketplace](https://github.com/ClaudeTools/marketplace/blob/HEAD/docs/src/content/docs/reference/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: PES 조사 단계에서 조사 항목 하나를 맡아 출처를 찾고 원문을 확인한 뒤 결과 JSON을 지정된 파일에 쓰고 경로와 한 줄 요약만 반환하는 서브에이전트. 메인이 지시문(주제·항목·조사 질문·이미 아는 것·확인한 출처·범위 밖·도구 규칙)을 빠짐없이 넘긴다. ([clazic/pes](https://github.com/clazic/pes/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))

- **researcher**: Research agent for multi-step tasks and broad codebase exploration. Use when a task requires web research combined with code reading, or when you are not confident a targeted search will find the right answer in the first few tries, or when exploration scope is genuinely unknown. Do NOT invoke simply because a task touches multiple files — if the file locations are known, use direct tool calls instead. One dispatch per question, several in parallel for broad topics. ([Mili-ssm/Agent-Skills](https://github.com/Mili-ssm/Agent-Skills/blob/HEAD/ports/copilot/agent-skills/com.github.copilot/agents/researcher.agent.md) · 2 copies)

Use before writing any feature over about 200 lines, before adding a dependency, and whenever a plan relies on a third-party fact. ([Milkeles/lectale](https://github.com/Milkeles/lectale/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Investigate a codebase or documentation and answer specific questions with evidence. Use in discovery mode before planning, or in targeted mode to resolve one question during execution. ([milkyware/awesome-ai](https://github.com/milkyware/awesome-ai/blob/HEAD/plugins/agent-workflow/.apm/agents/researcher.agent.md))

- **researcher**: Agente de descoberta read-only.
